18→|----------|-----------------|\n    19→| **First failure** | Saves error context to `last_failure.txt`, Claude retries knowing what broke |\n    20→| **Same error 3x** | Flags as pattern: \"You've changes |\n    22→| **15 failures on one story** | Circuit breaker trips, skips to next story, saves failure log for manual review |\n    23→\n    24→## Failure Context Accumulation\n    25→\n    26→Errors are **accumulated across retries**, not just the last failure:\n    27→\n    28→```\n    29→=== Attempt 1 failed for TASK-001 ===\n    30→ERROR: relation \"users\" does not exist\n    31→---\n    32→=== Attempt 2 failed for TASK-001 ===\n    33→ERROR: relation \"users\" does not exist\n    34→---\n    35→>>> STRUCTURAL ISSUE: Database schema mismatch\n    36→>>> ACTION NEEDED: \"$VERIFY_DIR/verify/api.sh\"\n    89→\n    90→run_verification() {\n    91→  local story=\"$1\"\n    92→\n    93→  echo \"\"\n    94→  print_info \"=== Verification: $story ===\"\n    95→  echo ============================================================================\n   219→# STRUCTURAL ERROR DETECTION\n   220→# ============================================================================\n   221→# Detects error patterns that indicate structural issues (not code bugs).\n   222→# These can't be fixed by This lets Claude see patterns:\n    33→#\n    34→#   === Attempt 1 failed for TASK-001 ===\n    35→#   ERROR: relation \"users\" does not exist\n    36→#   ---\n    37→#   === Attempt 2 failed for TASK-001 ===\n    38→#   ERROR: relation \"users\" does not exist\n    39→#   ---\n    40→#\n    41→# Seeing \"same error 3 times\" signals a structural issue (missing migration,\n    42→# wrong prerequisites) rather than a simple bug to fix.\n    43→#\n    44→# Context is:\n    45→#   - Appended per attempt (not overwritten)\n    46→#   - Capped at 200 lines to avoid huge prompts\n    47→#   - Cleared when switching to a new story\n    48→#   - Cleared on success\n    49→#\n    50→# STRUCTURAL ERROR DETECTION:\n    51→# Some errors indicate structural issues (not code bugs) that can't be fixed\n    52→# by retrying. ============================================================================\n   219→# STRUCTURAL ERROR DETECTION\n   220→# ============================================================================\n   221→# Detects error patterns that indicate structural issues (not code bugs).\n   222→# These can't be fixed by retrying - they need specific actions like DB reset.\n   223→#\n   224→_detect_structural_errors() {\n   225→  local context_file=\"$1\"\n   226→  [[ ! -f \"$context_file\" ]] && return\n   227→\n \">>> STRUCTURAL ISSUE: Database schema mismatch\" \"$context_file\" 2>/dev/null; then\n   235→    echo \"\"\n   236→    print_warning \"STRUCTURAL ISSUE DETECTED: Database schema mismatch\"\n   237→ code\"\n   262→      echo \">>> This is NOT a code bug - the test DB is missing schema changes\"\n   263→    } >> \"$context_file\"\n   264→  fi\n   265→\n   266→  # Migration pending errors\n   267→ This lets Claude see patterns:\n    33→#\n    34→#   === Attempt 1 failed for TASK-001 ===\n    35→#   ERROR: relation \"users\" does not exist\n    36→#   ---\n    37→#   === Attempt 2 failed for TASK-001 ===\n    38→#   ERROR: relation \"users\" does not exist\n    39→#   ---\n    40→#\n    41→# Seeing \"same error 3 times\" signals a structural issue (missing migration,\n    42→# wrong prerequisites) rather than a simple bug to fix.\n    43→#\n    44→# Context is:\n    45→#   - Appended per attempt (not overwritten)\n    46→#   - Capped at 200 lines to avoid huge prompts\n    47→#   - Cleared when switching to a new story\n    48→#   - Cleared on success\n    49→#\n    50→# STRUCTURAL ERROR DETECTION:\n    51→# Some errors indicate structural issues (not code bugs) that can't be fixed\n    52→# by retrying. ============================================================================\n   221→# Detects error patterns that indicate structural issues (not code bugs).\n   222→# These can't be fixed by markers on retry)\n   233→  if echo \"$error_content\" | grep -qiE \"(column.*does not exist|relation.*does not exist|no such column|unknown column|undefined column)\" && \\\n   234→     ! grep -q \">>> STRUCTURAL ISSUE: Database schema mismatch\" \"$context_file\" 2>/dev/null; then\n   235→    echo \"\"\n   236→    print_warning \"STRUCTURAL ISSUE DETECTED: Database schema mismatch\"\n   237→    echo \"\"\n   238→    echo \"  The test database is missing columns/tables that the code expects.\"\n   239→    echo \"  This usually happens when:\"\n   240→    echo \"    - Migrations were added code\"\n   262→      echo \">>> This is NOT a code bug - the test DB is missing schema changes\"\n   263→    } >> \"$context_file\"\n   264→  fi\n   265→\n   266→  # Migration pending errors\n   267→ ============================================================================\n   219→# STRUCTURAL ERROR DETECTION\n   220→# ============================================================================\n   221→# Detects error patterns that indicate structural issues (not code bugs).\n   222→# These can't be fixed by
